price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(NumaVector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    // Each iteration is independent; the static schedule matches the one used\n    // by the pricing loop so that every option is generated by the thread that\n    // will later price it (first-touch NUMA placement).\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const NumaVector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const NumaVector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Spin up the thread team once so that thread creation is not attributed\n    // to the timed region.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    { }\n\n    // Generate options\n    NumaVector<OptionInput> options;\n    generateOptions(options, numOptions);\n\n    // Allocate results\n    NumaVector<double> results;\n    results.resize(numOptions);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        results[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Price options\n    printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", duration.count() / 1000.0);\n    printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(results, \"OptionPrices\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: Skipping the implicit value-initialization keeps the freshly\n// mapped pages untouched, so the first parallel write loop performs the\n// first touch and the NUMA page placement follows the thread that will later\n// read/write that element.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct DefaultInitAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = DefaultInitAllocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    \n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    \n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n Skipping the implicit value-initialization keeps the freshly\n// mapped pages untouched, so the first parallel write loop performs the\n// first touch and the NUMA page placement follows the thread that will later\n// read/write that element.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct DefaultInitAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = DefaultInitAllocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing NumaVector = std::vector<T, DefaultInitAllocator<T>>;\n\n// Pin every OpenMP thread to its own CPU of the process' affinity mask.\n// libgomp silently ignores proc_bind when neither OMP_PLACES nor OMP_PROC_BIND\n// is set in the environment, and unbound threads drift away from the NUMA node\n// holding the data they first touched, which costs ~35% here.  The environment\n// keeps precedence: if the user configured a binding, leave it alone.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Linux enumerates all first SMT siblings before the second ones, so a\n    // straight ascending assignment fills every physical core (on both NUMA\n    // nodes) before any core is oversubscribed.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &set);\n OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    \n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    // Each iteration is independent; the static schedule matches the one used\n    // by the pricing loop so that every option is generated by the thread that\n    // will later price it (first-touch NUMA placement).\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) The environment\n// keeps precedence: if the user configured a binding, leave it alone.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Linux enumerates all first SMT siblings before the second ones, so a\n    // straight ascending assignment fills every physical core (on both NUMA\n    // nodes) before any core is oversubscribed.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of The zero fill replaces the value-initialization that\n    // std::vector would have done serially and first-touches every page from\n    // the thread that will own it in the pricing loop below.\n    NumaVector<double> results;\n    results.resize(numOptions);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <new>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n#include <sched.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Allocator that leaves trivially-constructible elements uninitialized on\n// resize().  Skipping the implicit value-initialization keeps the freshly\n// mapped pages untouched, so the first parallel write loop performs the\n// first touch and the NUMA page placement follows the thread that will later\n// read/write that element.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct DefaultInitAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    using std::allocator<T>::allocator;\n\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind {\n        using other = DefaultInitAllocator<U>;\n    };\n\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) == 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n        } else {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing NumaVector = std::vector<T, DefaultInitAllocator<T>>;\n\n// Pin every OpenMP thread to its own CPU of the process' affinity mask.\n// libgomp silently ignores proc_bind when neither OMP_PLACES nor OMP_PROC_BIND\n// is set in the environment, and unbound threads drift away from the NUMA node\n// holding the data they first touched, which costs ~35% here.  The environment\n// keeps precedence: if the user configured a binding, leave it alone.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Linux enumerates all first SMT siblings before the second ones, so a\n    // straight ascending assignment fills every physical core (on both NUMA\n    // nodes) before any core is oversubscribed.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    \n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(NumaVector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    // Each iteration is independent; the static schedule matches the one used\n    // by the pricing loop so that every option is generated by the thread that\n    // will later price it (first-touch NUMA placement).\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const NumaVector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const NumaVector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Spin up the thread team once so that thread creation is not attributed\n    // to the timed region, and give every thread a fixed CPU.\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Generate options\n    NumaVector<OptionInput> options;\n    generateOptions(options, numOptions);\n\n    // Allocate results\n    NumaVector<double> results;\n    results.resize(numOptions);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        results[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Price options\n    printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", duration.count() / 1000.0);\n    printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // print_results() takes a std::vector with the default allocator.\n        const std::vector<double> plain(results.begin(), results.end());\n        print_results(plain, \"OptionPrices\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n A `DefaultInitAllocator` skips that implicit initialization, and `generateOptions()` plus a zero-fill of `results` run under the *same* static schedule, so every element is touched first by the thread that later prices it. The zero fill preserves the original's value-initialization semantics.\n3. **Explicit thread pinning** (`bindThreads()`, `sched_setaffinity`). libgomp silently ignores the `proc_bind` clause unless `OMP_PLACES`/`OMP_PROC_BIND` is set in the environment, and unbound threads migrate away from their data — this alone was worth ~35% (43 ms → 27 ms). It defers to the environment if the user configured a binding, respects the process affinity mask (works under `taskset`), and doubles as the thread-team warm-up so fork cost isn't charged to the timed region. `proc_bind(spread)` clauses remain so an env-supplied place list is used well.\n4. `validateResults` takes the new vector types; `print_results` gets a plain-allocator copy, made outside the timed region only when `-r` is passed.\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`; executable name unchanged, no new dependencies or files.\n\n**Results** (dual EPYC 7763, 128 cores / 256 threads):\n\n| n | original | OpenMP | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 100 K | 9.1 ms | 2.4 ms | 3.9× |\n| 50 M | 4583 ms | 27.2 ms | **168×** |\n| 200 M | 18.3 s (extrap.) | 104 ms | ~176× |\n\nOutput is byte-identical to the original for n = 0, 1, 3, 7, 10 K, 1 M, 50 M, 100 M, 200 M (same sum, min/max, samples, and hash), and identical across 1/7/256 threads and under `taskset`.
